\"why\", \"how\",\n    33→        \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\", \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\",\n    34→        \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\", \"than\", \"too\", \"very\",\n    35→        \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\", \"because\", \"about\",\n    36→        \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\", \"below\",\n    37→        \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\", \"any\",\n    38→        \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n    39→        \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n    40→    ]\n    41→)\n    42→\n    43→TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n    44→\n    45→\n    46→def tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    47→    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    48→    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    49→    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n    50→\n    51→\n    52→def compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    53→    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    54→    if not tokens:\n    55→        return {}\n    56→    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    57→    for token in tokens:\n    58→        counts[token] += 1\n    59→    total = len(tokens)\n    60→    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n    61→\n    62→\n    63→# ============================================================================\n    64→# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n    65→# ============================================================================\n    66→\n    67→\n    68→@dataclass\n    69→class TFIDFIndex:\n    70→    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    71→    num_documents: int = 0\n    72→    vocabulary: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    73→    document_frequencies: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    74→    idf: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    75→    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    76→    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    77→    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    78→\n    79→\n    80→@dataclass\n    81→class SearchResult:\n    82→    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    83→    doc_id: int\n    84→    score: float\n    85→    title: str = \"\"\n    86→\n    87→\n    88→@dataclass\n    89→class ParallelIndexingResult:\n    90→    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n    91→    index: TFIDFIndex\n    92→    elapsed_time: float\n    93→    num_documents: int\n    94→    vocabulary_size: int\n    95→\n    96→\n    97→# ============================================================================\n    98→# Worker Functions for Parallel Processing\n    99→# ============================================================================\n   100→\n   101→# Global variables for worker processes (initialized via pool initializer)\n   102→_worker_index = None\n   103→_worker_doc_titles = None\n   104→\n   105→\n   106→def _init_search_worker(index_data, doc_titles_data):\n   107→    \"\"\"Initialize worker process with shared data.\"\"\"\n   108→    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n   109→    _worker_index = index_data\n   110→    _worker_doc_titles = doc_titles_data\n   111→\n   112→\n   113→def _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, set[str]], set[str]]:\n   114→    \"\"\"\n   115→    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n   116→\n   117→    Args:\n   118→        docs_data: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n   119→\n   120→    Returns:\n   121→        (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n   122→    \"\"\"\n   123→    doc_term_freqs = {}\n   124→    doc_terms = {}\n   125→    vocabulary = set()\n   126→\n   127→    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n   128→        text = title + \" \" + content\n   129→        tokens = tokenize(text)\n   130→        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n   131→        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n   132→        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n   133→        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n   134→\n   135→    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n   136→\n   137→\n   138→def _compute_df_for_terms(args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, set[str]]]) -> dict[str, int]:\n   139→    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a chunk of terms.\"\"\"\n   140→    terms_chunk, doc_terms = args\n   141→    df_results = {}\n   142→    for term in terms_chunk:\n   143→        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n   144→        df_results[term] = df\n   145→    return df_results\n   146→\n   147→\n   148→def _build_index_and_vectors_for_docs(args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n   149→    \"\"\"\n   150→    Build inverted index entries and document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n   151→\n   152→    This combines steps 4 and 5 from the sequential algorithm for efficiency.\n   153→\n   154→    Args:\n   155→        args: (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n   156→\n   157→    Returns:\n   158→        (partial_inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n   159→    \"\"\"\n   160→    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n   161→    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n   162→    doc_vectors = {}\n   163→    doc_norms = {}\n   164→\n   165→    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n   166→        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n   167→        doc_vector = {}\n   168→        norm_squared = 0.0\n   169→\n   170→        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n   171→            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n   172→            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n   173→            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   174→            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n   175→\n   176→        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n   177→        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n   178→\n   179→    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n   180→\n   181→\n   182→def _search_query_batch(queries_batch: list[tuple[str, int]]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n   183→    \"\"\"\n   184→    Search for a batch of queries using the global worker index.\n   185→\n   186→    Args:\n   187→        queries_batch: List of (query_string, query_index) tuples\n   188→\n   189→    Returns:\n   190→        List of (query_index, [(doc_id, score, title), ...]) tuples\n   191→    \"\"\"\n   192→    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n   193→    index = _worker_index\n   194→    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n   195→\n   196→    results = []\n   197→\n   198→    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n   199→        # Tokenize query\n   200→        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n   201→        if not query_tokens:\n   202→            results.append((query_idx, []))\n   203→            continue\n   204→\n   205→        # Compute query TF\n   206→        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n   207→\n   208→        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n   209→        query_vector = {}\n   210→        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   211→        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   212→            if term in index.idf:\n   213→                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   214→                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n   215→                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   216→\n   217→        if not query_vector:\n   218→            results.append((query_idx, []))\n   219→            continue\n   220→\n   221→        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n   222→\n   223→        # Find candidate documents\n   224→        candidate_docs = set()\n   225→        for term in query_vector:\n   226→            if term in index.inverted_index:\n   227→                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n   228→                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   229→\n   230→        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n   231→        scores = []\n   232→        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n   233→            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n   234→            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n   235→\n   236→            if doc_norm == 0:\n   237→                continue\n   238→\n   239→            # Dot product\n   240→            dot_product = sum(\n   241→                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n   242→                for term in query_vector\n   243→            )\n   244→\n   245→            # Cosine similarity\n   246→            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n   247→            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n   248→\n   249→        # Get top-10 results (hardcoded for now, will be passed properly)\n   250→        top_results = nlargest(10, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n   251→\n   252→        # Build result tuples\n   253→        query_results = []\n   254→        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n   255→            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n   256→            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n   257→\n   258→        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n   259→\n   260→    return results\n   261→\n   262→\n   263→def _search_query_batch_with_topk(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n   264→    \"\"\"\n   265→    Search for a batch of queries using the global worker index.\n   266→\n   267→    Args:\n   268→        args: (queries_batch, top_k) where queries_batch is List of (query_string, query_index) tuples\n   269→\n   270→    Returns:\n   271→        List of (query_index, [(doc_id, score, title), ...]) tuples\n   272→    \"\"\"\n   273→    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n   274→    queries_batch, top_k = args\n   275→    index = _worker_index\n   276→    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n   277→\n   278→    results = []\n   279→\n   280→    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n   281→        # Tokenize query\n   282→        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n   283→        if not query_tokens:\n   284→            results.append((query_idx, []))\n   285→            continue\n   286→\n   287→        # Compute query TF\n   288→        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n   289→\n   290→        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n   291→        query_vector = {}\n   292→        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n   293→        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n   294→            if term in index.idf:\n   295→                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n   296→                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n   297→                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n   298→\n   299→        if not query_vector:\n   300→            results.append((query_idx, []))\n   301→            continue\n   302→\n   303→        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n   304→\n   305→        # Find candidate documents\n   306→        candidate_docs = set()\n   307→        for term in query_vector:\n   308→            if term in index.inverted_index:\n   309→                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n   310→                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n   311→\n   312→        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n   313→        scores = []\n   314→        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n   315→            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n   316→            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n   317→\n   318→            if doc_norm == 0:\n   319→                continue\n   320→\n   321→            # Dot product\n   322→            dot_product = sum(\n   323→                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n   324→                for term in query_vector\n   325→            )\n   326→\n   327→            # Cosine similarity\n   328→            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n   329→            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n   330→\n   331→        # Get top-k results\n   332→        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n   333→\n   334→        # Build result tuples\n   335→        query_results = []\n   336→        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n   337→            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n   338→            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n   339→\n   340→        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n   341→\n   342→    return results\n   343→\n   344→\n   345→# ============================================================================\n   346→# Parallel Index Building\n   347→# ============================================================================\n   348→\n   349→\n   350→def build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n   351→    documents: list[Document],\n   352→    num_workers: int = None,\n   353→    chunk_size: int = 500\n   354→) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n   355→    \"\"\"\n   356→    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n   357→\n   358→    Parallelization strategy:\n   359→    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (chunk documents by size for load balancing)\n   360→    2. Merge vocabularies and compute document frequencies in parallel\n   361→    3. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n   362→    4. Parallel inverted index and document vector construction (chunk documents)\n   363→\n   364→    Args:\n   365→        documents: List of Document objects to index\n   366→        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n   367→        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n   368→\n   369→    Returns:\n   370→        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n   371→    \"\"\"\n   372→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   373→\n   374→    if num_workers is None:\n   375→        num_workers = cpu_count()\n   376→\n   377→    index = TFIDFIndex()\n   378→    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n   379→\n   380→    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n   381→    # Convert documents to lightweight tuples to reduce serialization overhead\n   382→    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n   383→\n   384→    # Split into chunks\n   385→    doc_batches = []\n   386→    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n   387→        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n   388→\n   389→    # Process chunks in parallel\n   390→    doc_term_freqs = {}\n   391→    doc_terms = {}\n   392→\n   393→    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n   394→        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n   395→\n   396→    # Merge results\n   397→    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n   398→        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n   399→        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n   400→        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n   401→\n   402→    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n   403→    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n   404→    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n   405→    term_chunks = []\n   406→    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n   407→        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n   408→\n   409→    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in term_chunks]\n   410→\n   411→    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n   412→        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, chunk_args)\n   413→\n   414→    for df_dict in df_results:\n   415→        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n   416→\n   417→    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n   418→    N = index.num_documents\n   419→    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n   420→        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n   421→\n   422→    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n   423→    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n   424→    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n   425→    doc_id_chunks = []\n   426→    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n   427→        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n   428→\n   429→    idf_dict = dict(index.idf)\n   430→    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n   431→\n   432→    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n   433→        combined_results = pool.map(_build_index_and_vectors_for_docs, chunk_args)\n   434→\n   435→    # Merge results\n   436→    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n   437→        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n   438→            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n   439→                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n   440→            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n   441→        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n   442→        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n   443→\n   444→    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correctness)\n   445→    for term in index.inverted_index:\n   446→        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n   447→\n   448→    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n   449→\n   450→    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n   451→        index=index,\n   452→        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n   453→        num_documents=len(documents),\n   454→        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n   455→    )\n   456→\n   457→\n   458→# ============================================================================\n   459→# Parallel Search\n   460→# ============================================================================\n   461→\n   462→\n   463→def batch_search_parallel(\n   464→    queries: list[str],\n   465→    index: TFIDFIndex,\n   466→    top_k: int = 10,\n   467→    num_workers: int = None,\n   468→    documents: list[Document] = None\n   469→) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n   470→    \"\"\"\n   471→    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n   472→\n   473→    Uses pool initializer to share index data once rather than serializing per query.\n   474→\n   475→    Args:\n   476→        queries: List of search query strings\n   477→        index: TF-IDF index to search\n   478→        top_k: Number of top results per query\n   479→        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n   480→        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n   481→\n   482→    Returns:\n   483→        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n   484→    \"\"\"\n   485→    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n   486→\n   487→    if num_workers is None:\n   488→        num_workers = cpu_count()\n   489→\n   490→    # Prepare document titles lookup\n   491→    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n   492→\n   493→    # Create indexed queries for result ordering\n   494→    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n   495→\n   496→    # Batch queries for each worker to reduce serialization overhead\n   497→    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n   498→    query_batches = []\n   499→    for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_worker):\n   500→        query_batches.append((indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_worker], top_k))\n   501→\n   502→    # Execute searches in parallel with shared index\n   503→    with Pool(\n   504→        processes=num_workers,\n   505→        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n   506→        initargs=(index, doc_titles)\n   507→    ) as pool:\n   508→        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch_with_topk, query_batches)\n   509→\n   510→    # Flatten and sort results by original query index\n   511→    all_results = []\n   512→    for batch in batch_results:\n   513→        all_results.extend(batch)\n   514→\n   515→    # Sort by query index and extract results\n   516→    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n   517→    final_results = []\n   518→    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n   519→        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n   520→                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n   521→        final_results.append(results)\n   522→\n   523→    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n   524→\n   525→    return final_results, elapsed\n   526→\n   527→\n   528→# ============================================================================\n   529→# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n   530→# ============================================================================\n   531→\n   532→\n   533→def main():\n   534→    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n   535→    import argparse\n   536→    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n   537→    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n   538→\n   539→    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n   540→    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n   541→    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\")\n   542→    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of parallel workers\")\n   543→    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n   544→\n   545→    args = parser.parse_args()\n   546→\n   547→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n   548→    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Comparison\")\n   549→    print(\"=\" * 60)\n   550→\n   551→    # Load or generate corpus\n   552→    if args.corpus:\n   553→        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n   554→        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n   555→    else:\n   556→        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n   557→        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n   558→\n   559→    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n   560→    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n   561→    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n   562→    print(f\"Workers: {args.num_workers}\")\n   563→\n   564→    # Sequential index building\n   565→    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n   566→    print(\"Building TF-IDF index (Sequential)...\")\n   567→    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n   568→    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n   569→\n   570→    # Parallel index building\n   571→    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (Parallel)...\")\n   572→    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n   573→        documents,\n   574→        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n   575→        chunk_size=args.chunk_size\n   576→    )\n   577→    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n   578→    print(f\"Speedup: {seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time:.2f}x\")\n   579→\n   580→    # \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_data: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_term_freqs, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries and document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This combines steps 4 and 5 from the sequential algorithm for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-10 results (hardcoded for now, will be passed properly)\n        top_results = nlargest(10, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build (query_string, query_index) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (query_index, [(doc_id, score, title), ...]) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result tuples\n        query_results = []\n ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (chunk documents by size for load balancing)\n    2. Merge vocabularies and compute document frequencies in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    4. Parallel inverted index and document vector construction (chunk documents)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # Convert documents to lightweight tuples to reduce serialization overhead\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Split into chunks\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n    term_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in term_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, chunk_args)\n\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    idf_dict = dict(index.idf)\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_index_and_vectors_for_docs, chunk_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correctness)\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses pool initializer to share index data once rather than serializing per query.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Batch queries for each worker to reduce serialization overhead\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_worker):\n        query_batches.append((indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_worker], top_k))\n\n    # all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    # Sort by query index and extract results\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = args\n    partial_index = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        posting_list = []\n        idf_val = idf_dict[term]\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_term_freqs.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf_val\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n        # Sort by score descending\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        partial_index[term] = posting_list\n\n    return partial_index\n\n\ndef _compute_doc_vectors_chunk(args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute document vectors and norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    Args:\n        args: (doc_ids_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_vectors, doc_norms) for the chunk\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids_chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids_chunk:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_single_query(args: tuple[str, TFIDFIndex, int, dict[int, str]]) -> list[SearchResult]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a single query. Worker function for parallel search.\n\n    Args:\n        args: (query, index, top_k, doc_titles)\n\n    Returns:\n        List of SearchResult objects\n    \"\"\"\n    query, index, top_k, doc_titles = args\n\n    # Tokenize query\n    query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n    if not query_tokens:\n        return []\n\n    # Compute query TF\n    query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n    # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n    query_vector = {}\n    query_norm_squared = 0.0\n    for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n        if term in index.idf:\n            tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n            query_vector[term] = tfidf\n            query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n    if not query_vector:\n        return []\n\n    query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n    # Find candidate documents\n    candidate_docs = set()\n    for term in query_vector:\n        if term in index.inverted_index:\n            for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n    # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n    scores = []\n    for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n        doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n        doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n        if doc_norm == 0:\n            continue\n\n        # Dot product\n        dot_product = sum(\n            query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n            for term in query_vector\n        )\n\n        # Cosine similarity\n        similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n        scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n    # Get top-k results\n    top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n    # Build result objects\n    results = []\n    for doc_id, score in top_results:\n        title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n        results.append(SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Steps:\n    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (chunk documents)\n    2. Merge vocabularies and compute document frequencies\n    3. Compute IDF scores\n    4. Parallel inverted index construction (chunk terms)\n    5. Parallel document vector and norm computation (chunk documents)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # Split documents into chunks\n    doc_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(documents), chunk_size):\n        doc_chunks.append(documents[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(_process_document_chunk, doc_chunks))\n\n    # Merge results\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies\n    # This is done sequentially as it requires aggregating across all documents\n    for term in index.vocabulary:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        index.document_frequencies[term] = df\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4: Parallel inverted index construction\n    # Split vocabulary into chunks\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers)\n    term_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n\n    # Prepare args for each chunk\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf)) for chunk in term_chunks]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        inv_idx_results = list(executor.map(_compute_inverted_index_chunk, chunk_args))\n\n    # Merge inverted index results\n    for partial_idx in inv_idx_results:\n        index.inverted_index.update(partial_idx)\n\n    # Step 5: Parallel document vector and norm computation\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers)\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    # Prepare args for each chunk\n    doc_chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf)) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        doc_vec_results = list(executor.map(_compute_doc_vectors_chunk, doc_chunk_args))\n\n    # Merge document vector results\n    for partial_vectors, partial_norms in doc_vec_results:\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare document titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Prepare args for each query\n    query_args = [(query, index, top_k, doc_titles) for query in queries]\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel\n    with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        results = list(executor.map(_search_single_query, query_args))\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of parallel workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Comparison\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n    term_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_df_worker, initargs=(doc_terms,)) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms_global, term_chunks)\n\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_index_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_index_and_vectors_global, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Create batches with more queries per batch to amortize overhead\n    queries_per_batch = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_batch):\n        query_batches.append((indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_batch], top_k))\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_search_worker, initargs=(index, doc_titles)) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_queries_global, query_batches)\n\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = ============================================================================\n# Global variables for worker processes (set via fork or initializer)\n# ============================================================================\n\n_global_doc_term_freqs = None\n_global_doc_terms = None\n_global_idf = None\n_global_index = None\n_global_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_df_worker(doc_terms):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker with doc_terms for DF computation.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_terms\n    _global_doc_terms = doc_terms\n\n\ndef _init_index_worker(doc_term_freqs, idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for inverted index and vector building.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    _global_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _global_idf = idf\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for search.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    _global_index = ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, set[str]], set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_data: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms_global(terms_chunk: list[str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a chunk of terms using global doc_terms.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_terms\n    doc_terms = _global_doc_terms\n    df_results = {}\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        df_results[term] = df\n    return df_results\n\n\ndef _build_index_and_vectors_global(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries and document vectors for a chunk of documents\n    using global doc_term_freqs and idf.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_queries_global(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n    term_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_df_worker, initargs=(doc_terms,)) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms_global, term_chunks)\n\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_index_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_index_and_vectors_global, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_data: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms(args: tuple[list[str], dict[int, set[str]]]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a chunk of terms.\"\"\"\n    terms_chunk, doc_terms = args\n    df_results = {}\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        df_results[term] = df\n    return df_results\n\n\ndef _build_index_and_vectors_for_docs(args: tuple[list[int], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[str, float]]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries and document vectors for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This combines steps 4 and 5 from the sequential algorithm for efficiency.\n\n    Args:\n        args: (doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict)\n\n    Returns:\n        (partial_inverted_index, doc_vectors, doc_norms)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_ids, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict = args\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch(queries_batch: list[tuple[str, int]]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using the global worker index.\n\n    Args:\n        queries_batch: List of (query_string, query_index) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (query_index, [(doc_id, score, title), ...]) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-10 results (hardcoded for now, will be passed properly)\n        top_results = nlargest(10, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result tuples\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\ndef _search_query_batch_with_topk(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using the global worker index.\n\n    Args:\n        args: (queries_batch, top_k) where queries_batch is List of (query_string, query_index) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        List of (query_index, [(doc_id, score, title), ...]) tuples\n    \"\"\"\n    global _worker_index, _worker_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _worker_index\n    doc_titles = _worker_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity for each candidate\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result tuples\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (chunk documents by size for load balancing)\n    2. Merge vocabularies and compute document frequencies in parallel\n    3. Compute IDF scores (sequential - fast)\n    4. Parallel inverted index and document vector construction (chunk documents)\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    # Convert documents to lightweight tuples to reduce serialization overhead\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Split into chunks\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    # Process chunks in parallel\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n    term_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_terms) for chunk in term_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms, chunk_args)\n\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    idf_dict = dict(index.idf)\n    chunk_args = [(chunk, doc_term_freqs, idf_dict) for chunk in doc_id_chunks]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_index_and_vectors_for_docs, chunk_args)\n\n    # Merge results\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correctness)\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Uses pool initializer to share index data once rather than serializing per query.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of result lists, elapsed_time)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    # Prepare document titles lookup\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    # Create indexed queries for result ordering\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Batch queries for each worker to reduce serialization overhead\n    queries_per_worker = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_worker):\n        query_batches.append((indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_worker], top_k))\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel with shared index\n    with Pool(\n        processes=num_workers,\n        initializer=_init_search_worker,\n        initargs=(index, doc_titles)\n    ) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_query_batch_with_topk, query_batches)\n\n    # Flatten and sort results by original query index\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    # Sort by query index and extract results\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of parallel workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Comparison\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n    print(f\"Workers: {args.num_workers}\")\n\n    # Sequential index building\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    print(\"Building TF-IDF index (Sequential)...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n    # Parallel index building\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (Parallel)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        chunk_size=args.chunk_size\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    print(\"Verifying correctness...\")\n    assert par_result.vocabulary_size == seq_result.vocabulary_size, \"Vocabulary size mismatch\"\n    assert par_result.num_documents == seq_result.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    print(\"✓ Vocabulary size matches\")\n    print(\"✓ Document count matches\")\n\n    # Test search\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    test_queries = [\n        \"machine learning algorithm\",\n        \"data analysis research\",\n        \"software development\",\n        \"medical treatment patient\",\n        \"business strategy investment\",\n    ] * 20  # 100 queries total\n\n    print(f\"Running {len(test_queries)} search queries...\")\n\n    # Sequential search\n    seq_start = time.perf_counter()\n    seq_search_results = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_result.index, top_k=10, documents=documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    par_search_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        test_queries,\n        par_result.index,\n        top_k=10,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel search time: {par_search_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Search speedup: {seq_search_time / par_search_time:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify search results match\n    print(\"\\nVerifying search results...\")\n    for i, (seq_res, par_res) in enumerate(zip(seq_search_results, par_search_results)):\n        for j, (s, p) in enumerate(zip(seq_res, par_res)):\n            assert s.doc_id == p.doc_id, f\"Query {i}, result {j}: doc_id mismatch\"\n            assert abs(s.score - p.score) < 1e-10, f\"Query {i}, result {j}: score mismatch\"\n    print(\"✓ All search results match\")\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"All tests passed!\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}
