      \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\", \"how\",\n        \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\", \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\",\n        \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\", \"than\", \"too\", \"very\",\n        \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\", \"because\", \"about\",\n        \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\", \"below\",\n        \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\", \"any\",\n        \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n        \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n    ]\n)\n\nTOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Global variables for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n_global_doc_term_freqs = None\n_global_doc_terms = None\n_global_idf = None\n_global_index = None\n_global_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_combined_worker(doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for combined DF and index building.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_doc_terms, _global_idf\n    _global_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _global_doc_terms = doc_terms\n    _global_idf = idf\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for search.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    _global_index = index\n    _global_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, set[str]], set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _compute_df_batch(terms: list[str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_terms\n    doc_terms = _global_doc_terms\n\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        count = 0\n        for term_set in doc_terms.values():\n            if term in term_set:\n                count += 1\n        result[term] = count\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_vectors_batch(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index and doc vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_batch(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a batch of queries.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (step 1)\n    2. Parallel document frequency computation (step 2)\n    3. Sequential IDF computation (fast)\n    4. Parallel inverted index and vector building (steps 4-5)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create balanced chunks based on document sizes for better load balancing\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Sequential DF computation (parallelizing this has too much overhead for modest vocab sizes)\n    # For very large vocabularies, we can parallelize, but for typical sizes it's faster sequential\n    if len(index.vocabulary) > 10000:\n        # Parallel DF for large vocabularies\n        vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n        term_chunks = [vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_combined_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, {})) as pool:\n            df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_batch, term_chunks)\n\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n    else:\n        # Sequential DF for smaller vocabularies\n        for term in index.vocabulary:\n            count = 0\n            for term_set in doc_terms.values():\n                if term in term_set:\n                    count += 1\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = count\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (always sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_combined_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_vectors_batch, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correct search)\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Batch queries - aim for balanced work per worker\n    queries_per_batch = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [(indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_batch], top_k)\n                     for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_batch)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_search_worker, initargs=(index, doc_titles)) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch, query_batches)\n\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point (for testing)\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Test the parallel implementation.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    from document_generator import generate_corpus, load_corpus\n    from sequential import build_tfidf_index_sequential, batch_search_sequential\n\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus JSON file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-docs\", type=int, default=5000, help=\"Number of documents to generate\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num-workers\", type=int, default=4, help=\"Number of parallel workers\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--chunk-size\", type=int, default=500, help=\"Chunk size for parallel processing\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine - Performance Comparison\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n    print(f\"Workers: {args.num_workers}\")\n\n    # Sequential index building\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    print(\"Building TF-IDF index (Sequential)...\")\n    seq_result = build_tfidf_index_sequential(documents)\n    print(f\"Sequential time: {seq_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n    # Parallel index building\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (Parallel)...\")\n    par_result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        chunk_size=args.chunk_size\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel time: {par_result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Speedup: {seq_result.elapsed_time / par_result.elapsed_time:.2f}x\")\n\n    # Verify correctness\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    print(\"Verifying correctness...\")\n    assert par_result.vocabulary_size == seq_result.vocabulary_size, \"Vocabulary size mismatch\"\n    assert par_result.num_documents == seq_result.num_documents, \"Document count mismatch\"\n    print(\"✓ Vocabulary size matches\")\n    print(\"✓ Document count matches\")\n\n    # Test search with more queries\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"-\" * 60)\n    test_queries = batch_search_sequential(test_queries, seq_result.index, top_k=10, documents=documents)\n    seq_search_time = time.perf_counter() - seq_start\n    print(f\"Sequential search time: {seq_search_time:.3f} seconds\")\n\n    # Parallel search\n    par_search_results, par_search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        test_queries,\n        par_result.index,\n        top_k=10,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents\n    )\n    print(f\"Parallel search time: {par_search_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Search speedup: {seq_search_time / re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = ============================================================================\n# Global variables for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n_global_doc_term_freqs = None\n_global_doc_terms = None\n_global_idf = None\n_global_index = None\n_global_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_combined_worker(doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for combined DF and index building.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_doc_terms, _global_idf\n    _global_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _global_doc_terms = doc_terms\n    _global_idf = idf\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, set[str]], set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _compute_df_batch(terms: list[str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_terms\n    doc_terms = _global_doc_terms\n\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        count = 0\n        for term_set in doc_terms.values():\n            if term in term_set:\n                count += 1\n        result[term] = count\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_vectors_batch(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index and doc vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_batch(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a batch of queries.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (step 1)\n    2. Parallel document frequency computation (step 2)\n    3. Sequential IDF computation (fast)\n    4. Parallel inverted index and vector building (steps 4-5)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create balanced chunks based on document sizes for better load balancing\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Sequential DF computation (parallelizing this has too much overhead for modest vocab sizes)\n    # For very large vocabularies, we can parallelize, but for typical sizes it's faster sequential\n    if len(index.vocabulary) > 10000:\n        # Parallel DF for large vocabularies\n        vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n        term_chunks = [vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_combined_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, {})) as pool:\n            df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_batch, term_chunks)\n\n        for df_dict in df_results:\n            index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n    else:\n        # Sequential DF for smaller vocabularies\n        for term in index.vocabulary:\n            count = 0\n            for term_set in doc_terms.values():\n                if term in term_set:\n                    count += 1\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = count\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (always sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_combined_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_vectors_batch, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correct search)\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Batch queries - aim for balanced work per worker\n    queries_per_batch = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [(indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_batch], top_k)\n                     for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_batch)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_search_worker, initargs=(index, doc_titles)) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch, query_batches)\n\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, set[str]], set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n\n    Args:\n        docs_data: List of (doc_id, title, content) tuples\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary)\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n        doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _compute_df_for_terms_global(terms_chunk: list[str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a chunk of terms using global doc_terms.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_terms\n    doc_terms = _global_doc_terms\n    df_results = {}\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = sum(1 for doc_id in doc_terms if term in doc_terms[doc_id])\n        df_results[term] = df\n    return df_results\n\n\ndef _build_index_and_vectors_global(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build inverted index entries and document vectors for a chunk of documents\n    using global doc_term_freqs and idf.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_queries_global(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for a batch of queries using global index.\n    \"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Number of documents per chunk for processing\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization and TF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_terms, chunk_vocab in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        doc_terms.update(chunk_terms)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n    # Step 2: Calculate document frequencies in parallel\n    vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n    term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n    term_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size):\n        term_chunks.append(vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size])\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_df_worker, initargs=(doc_terms,)) as pool:\n        df_results = pool.map(_compute_df_for_terms_global, term_chunks)\n\n    for df_dict in df_results:\n        index.document_frequencies.update(df_dict)\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 4 & 5: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size):\n        doc_id_chunks.append(doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size])\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_index_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_index_and_vectors_global, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (list of documents} if documents else {}\n\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Create batches with more queries per batch to amortize overhead\n x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# TF-IDF index building using multiprocessing\n2. Batch search using multiprocessing\n\nAchieves speedup on multi-core systems while maintaining\nidentical results to the sequential implementation.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport math\nimport re\nimport time\nfrom ============================================================================\n\nSTOP_WORDS = frozenset(\n    [\n        \"a\", \"an\", \"and\", \"are\", \"as\", \"at\", \"be\", \"by\", \"for\", \"from\",\n        \"has\", \"he\", \"in\", \"is\", \"it\", \"its\", \"of\", \"on\", \"that\", \"the\",\n        \"to\", \"was\", \"were\", \"will\", \"with\", \"the\", \"this\", \"but\", \"they\",\n        \"have\", \"had\", \"what\", \"when\", \"where\", \"who\", \"which\", \"why\", \"how\",\n        \"all\", \"each\", \"every\", \"both\", \"few\", \"more\", \"most\", \"other\", \"some\",\n        \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\", \"than\", \"too\", \"very\",\n        \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\", \"because\", \"about\",\n        \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\", \"below\",\n        \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\", \"any\",\n        \"do\", \"does\", \"did\", \"doing\", \"would\", \"could\", \"might\", \"must\",\n        \"shall\", \"may\", \"been\", \"being\", \"am\",\n    ]\n)\n\nTOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Global variables for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n_global_doc_term_freqs = None\n_global_idf = None\n_global_index = None\n_global_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_vectors_worker(doc_term_freqs, idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for vector building.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    _global_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _global_idf = idf\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for search.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    _global_index = index\n    _global_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], set[str], dict[str, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize, compute TF, and compute DF contribution.\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, df_contributions)\n        where df_contributions[term] = number of docs in this batch containing term\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n    df_contributions = defaultdict(int)\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n\n        # Track which terms appear in this document (for DF)\n        doc_vocab = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(doc_vocab)\n\n        # Count this document's contribution to DF\n        for term in doc_vocab:\n            df_contributions[term] += 1\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, dict(df_contributions)\n\n\ndef _build_vectors_batch(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index and doc vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_batch(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a batch of queries.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization, TF computation, and DF accumulation (combined)\n    2. Merge DF results and compute IDF (sequential - fast)\n    3. Parallel inverted index and vector building\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization, TF, and DF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create chunks\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    # Merge results from all batches\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_vocab, chunk_df in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        # Merge DF contributions\n        for term, count in chunk_df.items():\n            if term not in index.document_frequencies:\n                index.document_frequencies[term] = 0\n            index.document_frequencies[term] += count\n\n    # Step 2: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 3: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_vectors_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_vectors_batch, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correct search)\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Batch queries - aim for balanced work per worker\n    queries_per_batch = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [(indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_batch], top_k)\n                     for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_batch)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_search_worker, initargs=(index, doc_titles)) as pool:\n        batch_results = index=index,\n   326→        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n   327→        num_documents=len(documents),\n   328→        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n   329→    )\n   330→\n   331→\n   332→#  \"such\", \"no\", \"not\", \"only\", \"same\", \"so\", \"than\", \"too\", \"very\",\n        \"just\", \"can\", \"should\", \"now\", \"or\", \"if\", \"then\", \"because\", \"about\",\n        \"into\", \"through\", \"during\", \"before\", \"after\", \"above\", \"below\",\n        \"between\", \"under\", \"again\", \"further\", \"once\", \"here\", \"there\", \"any\",\n        \"do\", re.compile(r\"\\b[a-zA-Z]{2,}\\b\")\n\n\ndef tokenize(text: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Tokenize text into lowercase words, filtering stop words.\"\"\"\n    tokens = TOKEN_PATTERN.findall(text.lower())\n    return [t for t in tokens if t not in STOP_WORDS]\n\n\ndef compute_term_frequencies(tokens: list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n    num_documents: int = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ============================================================================\n# Global variables for worker processes\n# ============================================================================\n\n_global_doc_term_freqs = None\n_global_idf = None\n_global_index = None\n_global_doc_titles = None\n\n\ndef _init_vectors_worker(doc_term_freqs, idf):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for vector building.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    _global_doc_term_freqs = doc_term_freqs\n    _global_idf = idf\n\n\ndef _init_search_worker(index, doc_titles):\n    \"\"\"Initialize worker for search.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    _global_index = index\n    _global_doc_titles = doc_titles\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Worker Functions for Parallel Processing\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], set[str], dict[str, int]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize, compute TF, and compute DF contribution.\n\n    Returns:\n        (doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, df_contributions)\n        where df_contributions[term] = number of docs in this batch containing term\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n    df_contributions = defaultdict(int)\n\n    for doc_id, title, content in docs_data:\n        text = title + \" \" + content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_term_freqs[doc_id] = tf\n\n        # Track which terms appear in this document (for DF)\n        doc_vocab = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(doc_vocab)\n\n        # Count this document's contribution to DF\n        for term in doc_vocab:\n            df_contributions[term] += 1\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, vocabulary, dict(df_contributions)\n\n\ndef _build_vectors_batch(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index and doc vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id in doc_ids:\n        tf_dict = doc_term_freqs[doc_id]\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf_dict[term]\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n            partial_inverted_index[term].append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return dict(partial_inverted_index), doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _search_batch(args: tuple[list[tuple[str, int]], int]) -> list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a batch of queries.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization, TF computation, and DF accumulation (combined)\n    2. Merge DF results and compute IDF (sequential - fast)\n    3. Parallel inverted index and vector building\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n\n    # Step 1: Parallel tokenization, TF, and DF computation\n    docs_data = [(doc.doc_id, doc.title, doc.content) for doc in documents]\n\n    # Create chunks\n    doc_batches = []\n    for i in range(0, len(docs_data), chunk_size):\n        doc_batches.append(docs_data[i:i + chunk_size])\n\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:\n        results = pool.map(_process_document_batch, doc_batches)\n\n    # Merge results from all batches\n    for chunk_tf, chunk_vocab, chunk_df in results:\n        doc_term_freqs.update(chunk_tf)\n        index.vocabulary.update(chunk_vocab)\n\n        # Merge DF contributions\n        for term, count in chunk_df.items():\n            if term not in index.document_frequencies:\n                index.document_frequencies[term] = 0\n            index.document_frequencies[term] += count\n\n    # Step 2: Compute IDF scores (sequential - very fast)\n    N = index.num_documents\n    for term, df in index.document_frequencies.items():\n        index.idf[term] = math.log(N / df) + 1\n\n    # Step 3: Parallel inverted index and document vector construction\n    doc_ids = list(doc_term_freqs.keys())\n    doc_chunk_size = max(1, len(doc_ids) // num_workers + 1)\n    doc_id_chunks = [doc_ids[i:i + doc_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(doc_ids), doc_chunk_size)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_vectors_worker, initargs=(doc_term_freqs, dict(index.idf))) as pool:\n        combined_results = pool.map(_build_vectors_batch, doc_id_chunks)\n\n    for partial_idx, partial_vectors, partial_norms in combined_results:\n        for term, postings in partial_idx.items():\n            if term not in index.inverted_index:\n                index.inverted_index[term] = []\n            index.inverted_index[term].extend(postings)\n        index.doc_vectors.update(partial_vectors)\n        index.doc_norms.update(partial_norms)\n\n    # Sort posting lists by score descending (required for correct search)\n    for term in index.inverted_index:\n        index.inverted_index[term].sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(index.vocabulary)\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel.\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n\n    indexed_queries = [(query, idx) for idx, query in enumerate(queries)]\n\n    # Batch queries - aim for balanced work per worker\n    queries_per_batch = max(1, len(indexed_queries) // num_workers + 1)\n    query_batches = [(indexed_queries[i:i + queries_per_batch], top_k)\n                     for i in range(0, len(indexed_queries), queries_per_batch)]\n\n    with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_search_worker, initargs=(index, doc_titles)) as pool:\n        batch_results = pool.map(_search_batch, query_batches)\n\n    all_results = []\n    for batch in batch_results:\n        all_results.extend(batch)\n\n    all_results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])\n    final_results = []\n    for query_idx, result_tuples in all_results:\n        results = [SearchResult(doc_id=doc_id, score=score, title=title)\n                   for doc_id, score, title in result_tuples]\n        final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n# frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# TF-IDF Index Data Structures\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _process_document_batch(docs_data: list[tuple[int, str, str]]) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, set[str]], set[str]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Process a batch of documents: tokenize and compute term frequencies.\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_term_freqs = {}\n    doc_terms = {}\n    vocabulary = set()\n\n    for   doc_terms[doc_id] = set(tf.keys())\n        vocabulary.update(tf.keys())\n\n    return doc_term_freqs, doc_terms, vocabulary\n\n\ndef _compute_df_batch(terms: list[str]) -> dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"Compute document frequencies for a batch of terms.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_terms\n    doc_terms = _global_doc_terms\n\n    result = {}\n    for term in terms:\n        count = 0\n        for term_set in doc_terms.values():\n            if term in term_set:\n                count += 1\n        result[term] = count\n    return result\n\n\ndef _build_vectors_batch(doc_ids: list[int]) -> tuple[dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]], dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"Build inverted index and doc vectors for a batch of documents.\"\"\"\n    global _global_doc_term_freqs, _global_idf\n    doc_term_freqs = _global_doc_term_freqs\n    idf_dict = _global_idf\n\n    partial_inverted_index = defaultdict(list)\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = list[tuple[int, list[tuple[int, float, str]]]]:\n    \"\"\"Search for a batch of queries.\"\"\"\n    global _global_index, _global_doc_titles\n    queries_batch, top_k = args\n    index = _global_index\n    doc_titles = _global_doc_titles\n\n    results = []\n\n    for query, query_idx in queries_batch:\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append((query_idx, []))\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        candidate_docs = set()\n = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        query_results = []\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            title = doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\")\n            query_results.append((doc_id, score, title))\n\n        results.append((query_idx, query_results))\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# 500\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    Parallelization strategy:\n    1. Parallel tokenization and TF computation (step 1)\n    2. Parallel document frequency computation (step 2)\n    3. Sequential IDF computation (fast)\n    4. Parallel inverted index and vector building (steps 4-5)\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = cpu_count()\n\n    index = computation (parallelizing this has too much overhead for modest vocab sizes)\n    # For very large vocabularies, we can parallelize, but for typical sizes it's faster sequential\n    if len(index.vocabulary) > 10000:\n        # Parallel DF for large vocabularies\n        vocab_list = list(index.vocabulary)\n        term_chunk_size = max(1, len(vocab_list) // num_workers + 1)\n        term_chunks = [vocab_list[i:i + term_chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(vocab_list), term_chunk_size)]\n\n        with Pool(processes=num_workers, initializer=_init_combined_worker, doc_terms.values():\n                if term in term_set:\n                    count += 1\n            index.document_frequencies[term] = count\n\n    # Step 3: Compute IDF scores (always sequential - ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], final_results.append(results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return final_results, elapsed\n\n\n#
